recon).pow(2).mean().sqrt()\norig_norm = embed_weight.pow(2).mean().sqrt()\nprint(f\"Embedding 4-bit quantization RMSE: {err:.6f} (relative: {err/orig_norm:.6f})\")\n\ndel fp16_model\ntorch.cuda.empty_cache()\n\nprint(\"Loading AWQ quantized weights...\")\nawq_weights = load_file(os.path.join(AWQ_DIR, \"model.safetensors\"))\n\ncustom_weights = {}\nfor k, v in awq_weights.items():\n    if k == \"model.embed_tokens.weight\":\n        custom_weights[\"embed_packed_uint8\"] = embed_uint8\n        custom_weights[\"embed_scales\"] = embed_scales\n    else:\n        custom_weights[k] = v\n\nsave_path = os.path.join(CUSTOM_DIR, \"model.safetensors\")\nsave_file(custom_weights, save_path)\n\norig_size = os.path.getsize(os.path.join(AWQ_DIR, \"model.safetensors\"))\nnew_size = os.path.getsize(save_path)\nprint(f\"Original AWQ size: {orig_size:,} bytes ({orig_size/1e9:.2f} GB)\")\nprint(f\"Custom v2 size: {new_size:,} bytes ({new_size/1e9:.2f} GB)\")\nprint(f\"Saved: {(orig_size - Qwen3-4B v2: AWQ 4-bit linear + 4-bit embedding with per-group scales.\"\"\"\nimport os\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=DeprecationWarning)\n\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\n\n_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nCUSTOM_DIR = os.path.join(_DIR, \"qwen3-4b-custom-v2\")\nAWQ_DIR = os.path.join(_DIR, \"qwen3-4b-awq-4bit\")\n_SAFETENSORS = os.path.join(CUSTOM_DIR, \"model.safetensors\")\n\nGROUP_SIZE = 128\n\n\nclass QuantizedEmbedding4bit(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, packed_uint8, scales):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.register_buffer(\"packed_uint8\", packed_uint8)\n        self.register_buffer(\"scales\", scales)\n        self.vocab_size = packed_uint8.shape[0]\n        self.hidden_dim = packed_uint8.shape[1] * 2\n\n    def _unpack_rows(self, indices):\n        packed = self.packed_uint8[indices]\n        low = (packed & 0x0F).to(torch.int8)\n        low = low - (low >= 8).to(torch.int8) * 16\n        high = ((packed >> 4) & 0x0F).to(torch.int8)\n        high = high - (high >= 8).to(torch.int8) * 16\n\n        B = indices.shape\n        n_groups = self.hidden_dim // GROUP_SIZE\n        low = low.reshape(*B, n_groups, GROUP_SIZE // 2)\n        high = high.reshape(*B, n_groups, GROUP_SIZE // 2)\n        interleaved = torch.stack([low, high], dim=-1).reshape(*B, n_groups, GROUP_SIZE)\n\n        s = self.scales[indices].unsqueeze(-1)\n        return (interleaved.float() * s.float() / 7.0).reshape(*B, self.hidden_dim)\n\n    def forward(self, input_ids):\n        return self._unpack_rows(input_ids)\n\n\ndef load_model(device=\"cuda\"):\n    from awq import AutoAWQForCausalLM\n    from safetensors.torch import load_file\n\n    awq_model = ref_ppl) / ref_ppl\n    quality = math.exp(-max(0, delta))\n    ratio = 8044936192 / size\n    fitness = quality * math.log2(ratio)\n    print(f'{name}: delta_ppl={delta*100:.2f}%, quality={quality:.4f}, log2(ratio)={math.log2(ratio):.3f}, fitness={fitness:.4f}')\n\" 2>&1","workingDirectory":"","exitCode":0,"signal":"","stdout":"/home/infatoshi/.local/lib/python3.12/site-packages/awq/__init__.py:21: DeprecationWarning: \nI have left this message as the final dev message to help you transition.\n\nImportant Notice:\n- AutoAWQ is officially deprecated and will no longer be maintained.\n- The last tested configuration used Torch 2.6.0 and Transformers 4.51.3.\n- If future versions of Transformers break AutoAWQ compatibility, please report the issue to the Transformers project.\n\nAlternative:\n- AutoAWQ has been adopted by the vLLM Project: https://github.com/vllm-project/llm-compressor\n\nFor further inquiries, feel free to reach out:\n- X: https://x.com/casper_hansen_\n- LinkedIn: https://www.linkedin.com/in/casper-hansen-804005170/\n\n  warnings.warn(_FINAL_DEV_MESSAGE, category=DeprecationWarning, stacklevel=1)\n/home/infatoshi/.local/lib/python3.12/site-packages/torch/jit/_script.py:1488: DeprecationWarning: `torch.jit.script` is deprecated. Let me verify the packing code:\n\nLooking at the packing code:\n```python\nembed_packed = embed_int4.reshape(V, n_groups, GROUP_SIZE // 2, 2)\nlow = embed_packed[:, :, :, 0].to(torch.uint8) & 0x0F\nhigh = (embed_packed[:, :, :, 1].to(torch.uint8) & 0x0F) << 4\n```\n\nAnd the unpacking:\n```python\nlow = (packed & 0x0F).to(torch.int8)\nhigh = ((packed >> 4) & 0x0F).to(torch.int8)\ninterleaved = torch.stack([low, high], dim=-1).reshape(V, n_groups, GROUP_SIZE)\n```\n\nWait, the issue is that the packing code reshapes as `(V, n_groups, GROUP_SIZE // 2, 2)` where the last dim alternates [elem0, elem1]. Then `low` takes elem0 and `high` takes elem1. In unpacking, I stack them as `[low, high]` in the last dim, which produces `[elem0, elem1]` pairs. That looks correct...\n\nBut the actual problem might be simpler - the scale computation is wrong. Let me check: `group_absmax / 7.0` is the scale, and reconstruction should be `int4_val * scale = int4_val * group_absmax / 7.0`. The values are in range [-8, 7]. We could strip it out and save only what we need.\n\nWait! Looking at the file structure: the custom safetensors file still loads all AWQ weights from the AWQ_DIR. The AWQ model loads from AWQ_DIR and then we replace the embedding with the custom data.\n\nThis means:\n1. The AWQ model loads its full safetensors including the fp16 embedding\n2. Then we replace the embedding with our INT8 version\n\nThis is inefficient in two ways:\n- Loading: loads the AWQ fp16 embedding unnecessarily\n- Disk: both AWQ_DIR and CUSTOM_DIR exist on disk\n\nBut for the evaluation, only `get_compressed_size()` matters. We return the size of the custom safetensors which has the INT8 embedding + AWQ linear weights (no fp16 embedding). F.cross_entropy(shift.view(-1, shift.size(-1)).float(), labels.view(-1), reduction='sum')\n            total_loss += loss.item()\n            total_tokens += labels.numel()\n    return sum(p.data.nbytes for _, p in model_8bit.named_parameters())\n\nref_ppl = 3.4915\ndelta = (ppl - ref_ppl) / ref_ppl\nquality = math.exp(-max(0, delta))\nratio = 8044936192 / total_bytes\nfitness =
